Top-level globals:
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*dx)

def project(h,n):
    y=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    target=n/2.0
    # Bisection for tau. To reduce iterations, start with current sum.
    low=-1.0
    high=1.0
    # If y maybe outside, clip? We'll do y clip? For projection to box from y, use clip(y+tau). If y not in [0,1], still okay but tau range maybe need wider. We'll clip y to [-1,2]? Better set low = min(np.min(y),0)-1, high=max(np.max(y),1)+1. But y from feasible approx. 
    # We can compute using np.clip in loop.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(low+high)/2
       s=np.clip(y+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s<target: low=mid else high=mid
    h=np.clip(y+(low+high)/2,0,1)
    # final exact correction? sum error maybe due to numeric. Use small correction uniformly among free? 
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff !=0:
       # if |diff| small, adjust non-bound; if large (due to tau precision), iterate more or use scaling.
       free=(h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14)
       if free.any():
          hf=h.copy(); hf[free]+=diff/free.sum(); h=np.clip(hf,0,1)
       # if still off, use iterative few
       for _ in range(20): ...
    return h
```
Bisection 60 gives sum precision ~n/2^60 ~1e-15, okay. O(60n) fine.
But if y is exactly feasible, this changes by tiny tau maybe 0; okay.

Correct coordinate descent:
```
def cd_update(c,h,p,n):
    # returns new x, A, diag_idx?
    k1_start = p - n + 1
    k1_end = p
    k2_start = -p
    k2_end = n - 1 - p
    k_min = min(k1_start,k2_start)
    k_max = max(k1_end,k2_end)
    t_min = k_min + n - 1
    t_max = k_max + n - 1
    Lr = k_max - k_min + 1
    A = np.zeros(Lr)
    # I1 add 1-h[p-k], k from max(k_min,k1_start) to min(k_max,k1_end)
    s=max(k_min,k1_start); e=min(k_max,k1_end)
    if s<=e:
       off_s=s-k_min; off_e=e-k_min
       # j=p-k; when k=s, j=p-s; when k=e, j=p-e
       j_start=p-e; j_end=p-s # increasing? If we want corresponding order from k=s to e: j = p-s down to p-e. 
       # A[off_s:off_e+1] += 1 - h[p-s : p-e+1][::-1]? 
       vec = h[p-s : p-e+1][::-1]  # length e-s+1, starts j=p-s ends j=p-e
       A[off_s:off_e+1] += 1.0 - vec
    # I2 add -h[p+k]
    s=max(k_min,k2_start); e=min(k_max,k2_end)
    if s<=e:
       off_s=s-k_min; off_e=e-k_min
       vec = h[p+s : p+e+1]
       A[off_s:off_e+1] -= vec
    c_seg = c[t_min:t_max+1]
    t_diag = n-1
    diag_off = t_diag - t_min
    # Note if diag within range (should always because k=0 union? Yes)
    h0=h[p]
    # solve x
```

def exact_cd(h,n,passes,deadline,rng):
    h=h.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,full); best=compute_c5? track cmax*dx.
    for pass in range(passes):
       if time: break
       improved=False
       order=... maybe random or weighted.
       for p in order:
          if time > deadline: break
          if h[p] <=1e-13 or >=1-1e-13: continue
          h0=h[p]
          # build A,c_seg,diag, unaff
          # if global max maybe unaffected? We can quick bound: if current c maximum outside and no affected can lower below? Need solve.
          xn=solve_1d_seg(...)
          if abs(xn-h0)>1e-9:
             old_max=c.max() maybe maintain
             update c
             new_max=c.max()
             if new_max < old_max - 1e-12:
                 h[p]=xn; improved=True
             else:
                 # revert c
                 c -= delta*A? delta = xn-h0; c -= delta*A; c[diag]+=delta^2? if not accept.
       if not improved: break
       h=project(h,n) # if sum drift; after projection c should recompute to be exact
       # recompute c after projection maybe if drift significant; else update? Projection changes all h; easier recompute c each pass.
```

```
def solve_segment(A_lin, c_seg, A_diag, c_diag, h0, unaff, iters=24):
    # A_lin has diag set 0.
    # initial L_hi = max(unaff, c_seg.max(), 0)
    lo_all = -h0; hi_all = 1.0 - h0
    Llo = unaff
    Lhi = max(unaff, float(c_seg.max()), 0.0)
    # If Lhi - Llo small, x=h0.
    pos = A_lin > 1e-14
    neg = A_lin < -1e-14
    zero = np.abs(A_lin) <= 1e-14
    # Precompute c arrays? c_seg, A.
    for _ in range(iters):
       L = 0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       if L < unaff: Lhi=L? Actually Llo=unaff so no.
       # Check zero
       if zero.any() and c_seg[zero].max() > L + 1e-12:
           Llo=L; continue
       lo, hi = lo_all, hi_all
       if pos.any():
           ub = (L - c_seg[pos]) / A_lin[pos]
           u = np.max(ub)
           if u < hi: hi = u
       if neg.any():
           lb = (L - c_seg[neg]) / A_lin[neg]
           l = np.max(lb)
           if l > lo: lo = l
       if lo > hi + 1e-12:
           Llo=L; continue
       lo = max(lo, lo_all); hi = min(hi, hi_all)
       if lo > hi + 1e-12:
           Llo=L; continue
       # diag
       feas = True
       if diag exists:
          D = A_diag*A_diag - 4.0*(L - c_diag)
          if D >= 0.0:
             rt=np.sqrt(D); r1=0.5*(A_diag-rt); r2=0.5*(A_diag+rt)
             if lo > r1 and hi < r2:  # entirely forbidden
                 feas=False
          # if D<0 all feasible
       if feas: Lhi=L else Llo=L
       if Lhi-Llo < 1e-7: break
    L = Lhi + 1e-12
    # find delta
    lo,hi = lo_all,hi_all
    if pos.any(): hi=min(hi, np.max((L-c_seg[pos])/A_lin[pos]))
    if neg.any(): lo=max(lo, np.max((L-c_seg[neg])/A_lin[neg]))
    lo=max(lo,lo_all); hi=min(hi,hi_all)
    if lo > hi:
       # try delta 0 if feasible? 
       # We can return 0 if current f <= Lhi? 
       # Or pick midpoint clipped.
       if lo_all <= hi_all:
           # maybe due to numeric; choose projection of 0
           if c_seg[zero].max() <= Lhi and ...
       return 0.0
    D=...
    if D < 0:
       candidates=[(lo,hi)]
    else:
       r1,r2
       cands=[]
       if lo <= r1: cands.append((lo,min(hi,r1)))
       if hi >= r2: cands.append((max(lo,r2),hi))
       if not cands:
          # whole interval inside forbidden; due to numeric, return 0
          return 0.0
       # choose candidate containing lo? choose max length
       # Could choose candidate with largest length; if tie closer to 0.
    delta=a? maybe to reduce movement, choose point in candidate closest to 0 clipped.
```

def cd_pass(h,c, deadline, rng, full=True, skip_tol=1e-10):
   n=len(h); Lc=len(c); improved=False; cmax=c.max()
   order = list(range(n)) maybe shuffle
   for p in order:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      h0=h[p]
      if h0 <=1e-12 or h0 >=1-1e-12: continue
      # quick skip if unaff >= cmax - skip_tol:
      # but need t_min/t_max. Compute k range.
      k_min=...; k_max=...
      t_min=...; t_max=...
      if t_min>0 or t_max<Lc-1:
         if t_min>0: ma=np.max(c[:t_min])
         else: ma=-np.inf
         if t_max+1<Lc:
             mb=np.max(c[t_max+1:])
             ma=max(ma,mb)
         if ma >= cmax - 1e-8:
             # if no active max affected, skip
             # But if ma < cmax and cmax inside segment, can improve.
             continue
      # build A etc
      A=...
      c_seg_view = c[t_min:t_max+1]
      # Need copy? A_lin = A.copy; diag set 0.
      # unaff exact computed above; maybe recompute? ma.
      delta = solve_segment(...)
      if abs(delta)>1e-10:
         old_max=cmax
         Afull? A only segment.
         c[t_min:t_max+1] += delta*A
         c[n-1] -= delta*delta  # ensuring diag in segment
         new_max=float(c.max())
         if new_max < old_max - 1e-11:
             h[p]=h0+delta
             cmax=new_max
             improved=True
         else:
             c[t_min:t_max+1] -= delta*A
             c[n-1] += delta*delta
   return h,c,cmax,improved
```
But `c[n-1]` update: The diagonal lag index n-1 may be inside segment. We already included `delta*A` at diag, A_diag=1-2h0. The full change for diag is delta*(1-2h0)-delta^2, correct. So after adding segment, subtract delta^2 at n-1. If segment update already included A_diag from I1+I2. Good.
When reverting, add delta^2.
If we compute unaff with cmax stale? cmax updated only accepted. If we revert, cmax unchanged. Good.
If we skipped based on cmax maybe cmax outside not exact if previous coordinate increased? We only accept decreases, so cmax nonincreasing within pass. Good.

But the skip using `ma >= cmax - 1e-8` can be too aggressive if cmax is inside segment but ma equal cmax-1e-9; then no reduction below cmax-1e-9 impossible; okay. If due to cmax not exact? cmax from c.max previous. Good.
Potential issue: If max c has multiple active, and coordinate affects one, but another max unaffected, ma=cmax, skip; indeed cannot reduce max. It could change value of affected max below cmax, leaving other max; no improvement. Good.

We need recompute c after projection/exact each pass. In `cd_optimizer`, run passes until no improvement or time.
```
def cd_optimize(h,n,passes,deadline,rng):
    h=h.copy(); c=corr(h); best_c=compute_c5
    for it in range(passes):
       if time: break
       improved = cd_pass(...)
       if not improved: break
       # after pass, projection maybe changes; recompute c
       h=project(h,n)
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       c5=...
       update best

```
def _set_state(n,alpha,target): global _N,_ALPHA,_TARGET; _N=n; _ALPHA=alpha; _TARGET=target
def _obj(x):
    n=_N; a=_ALPHA
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full'); m=float(c.max()); sh=a*(c-m); s=float(np.exp(sh).sum()); return m - np.log(s)/a
def _jac(x):
    n=_N; a=_ALPHA
    c=...; m=...; sh=...; exps=np.exp(sh); s=exps.sum(); w=exps/s
    L=2*n-1
    # sliding sum w over w[p:p+n] for p 0..n-1: use cumsum
    cw=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
    aconst=cw[n:cw.size]? Need length n: sum w[p:p+n] for p=0..n-1. cw[i+1]-cw[i]. Use:
    aconst = cw[1:n+1] - cw[0:n]? Wait if w length L=2n-1. For p=0: sum w[0:n], p=n-1: sum w[n-1:2n-1]. Use cw[c_end]-cw[c_start]. c_start=p, c_end=p+n. Since p+n max 2n-1=L. aconst = cw[n: 2*n] - cw[0:n-1]? Let's make:
    starts=np.arange(n); ends=starts+n; aconst=cw[ends]-cw[starts]. This creates np.arange(n), okay.
    T1=np.convolve(x,w,mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    wrev=w[::-1]
    T2=np.convolve(x,wrev,mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    g=aconst - T1 - T2
    return g
```

Function:
```
def cd_optimize(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng=None,bisect_iters=24):
    h=h.copy(); c=corr(h); best=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy():
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       h,c,cmax,improved = cd_pass(h,c,bisect_iters,deadline,rng)
       if not improved: break
       # project if sum off
       if abs(h.sum()-n/2)>1e-8:
          h=project(h,n)
          c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       else:
          # sum may still drift by small; project anyway? Use project always at end for consistency.
          h=project(h,n)
          c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       cur=compute_c5(h,n)
       if cur<best: best=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best
```

A for coordinate p:

A(p,k)=I_base k∈[-p,n-1-p] (1-h_{p+k}) + I_shift k∈[p-n+1,p] (-h_{p-k}).
Grad = const window k∈[-p,n-1-p] - Σ_{k in window} w_k h_{p+k} - Σ_{k∈[p-n+1,p]} w_k h_{p-k}.
First conv part: k∈[-p,n-1-p], j=p+k => j 0..n-1, k=j-p, term h_j w_{j-p} = T2 (conv with Wrev). Second: k∈[p-n+1,p], j=p-k, term h_j w_{p-j} = T1 (conv with W). Constant indices: k start -p => idx n-1-p, end n-1-p? end n-1-p? Wait end k=n-1-p => idx 2n-2-p. Start k=-p => idx n-1-p. Length n: W[n-1-p : n-1-p+n] = [n-1-p : 2n-1-p]. For p 0: indices n-1..2n-2; for p=n-1: 0..n-1.
So orientation flag needed.
For D2, Aconst = sliding sum over W indices [n-1-p, 2n-1-p]. T1/T2 maybe grad = Aconst - T2? Let's map:
Term1 conv: -T2 (j-p). Term2 conv: -T1 (p-j). So same grad = Aconst - T1 - T2 (because subtraction symmetric). Yes, T1 and T2 defined as before. Thus only Aconst window differs. Diagonal? For solve A segments, formulas differ in ranges and signs. We can branch.

For coordinate A in solve:
If orient D1 (`_ORIENT==0`):
 intervals: I1=[p-n+1,p] add 1-h[p-k]; I2=[-p,n-1-p] add -h[p+k].
If orient D2 (`_ORIENT==1`):
 intervals: I1=[-p,n-1-p] add 1-h[p+k]; I2=[p-n+1,p] add -h[p-k].
This can be unified by a function that given p returns two (start,end, type) where type +1 for 1-h[p-k] (k interval with j=p-k decreasing) and -1 for -h[p+k] (j=p+k increasing). For D2, + interval uses j=p+k (increasing) not p-k. So two helper patterns.

def add_plus_decreasing(s,e): # coeff 1 - h[p-k]

def add_min_increasing(s,e): # coeff -h[p+k]
   vec=h[p+s:p+e+1]; A -= vec
```

Implementation:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    y=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    target=n/2.0
    # If y already clipped?
    low=-2.0; high=2.0
    # find tau such that sum clip(y+tau)=target.
    # sum at low maybe 0 if y+low <0 all? If y has values >2, not. Use yclip = np.clip(y,0,1) to be safe.
    y=np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)
    if y.sum()==target: return y
    # determine low/high: min_y -1, max_y -1? y in [0,1], tau in [-1,1] gives sums 0,n. Use [-1,1].
    low, high = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(low+high)/2
       s=np.clip(y+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s < target: low=mid else: high=mid
    h=np.clip(y+(low+high)/2,0.0,1.0)
    # fix residual
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff !=0:
       # adjust free
       for _ in range(10):
          free=(h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14)
          if not free.any(): break
          h[free]=np.clip(h[free]+diff/free.sum(),0,1)
          diff=target-h.sum()
          if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
    return h
```
If h sum off due to numerical bisection tiny, free adjust okay. But if many at bounds and diff not small, maybe not exact; rare.
Could use sorted water-fill for exact, but bisection 60 enough double precision. Sum of 700 values; sum error <1e-12.

```
def cd_coefficients(p,h):
  n=h.size
  k1s=p-n+1; k1e=p
  k2s=-p; k2e=n-1-p
  kmin=min(k1s,k2s); kmax=max(k1e,k2e)
  A=np.zeros(kmax-kmin+1)
  if _ORIENT==0:
     s=max(kmin,k1s); e=min(kmax,k1e)
     if s<=e: A[s-kmin:e-kmin+1] += 1.0 - h[p-s:p-e+1][::-1]
     s=max(kmin,k2s); e=min(kmax,k2e)
     if s<=e: A[s-kmin:e-kmin+1] -= h[p+s:p+e+1]
  else:
     # plus inc on k2
     s=max(kmin,k2s); e=min(kmax,k2e)
     if s<=e: A[...] += 1.0 - h[p+s:p+e+1]
     # minus dec on k1
     s=max(kmin,k1s); e=min(kmax,k1e)
     if s<=e: A[...] -= h[p-s:p-e+1][::-1]
  tmin=kmin+n-1; tmax=kmax+n-1
  return tmin,tmax,A
```
Need ensure slices not empty and within bounds. For plus_dec: h[p-s : p-e+1] where p-s is max index? Example D1 p=0,n=5 k1s=-4,k1e=0, s=-4,e=0: h[0:5][::-1] good. For plus_inc: h[p+s:p+e+1], p=0 k2s=0,k2e=4: h[0:5]. For p=4 D1 k1s=0,k1e=4: s=0,e=4: h[4:1][::-1]? p-e+1=1, slice start 4 stop 1 empty! Because p-s=4, p-e+1=1, but we want j=p-k for k=0..4: j=4,3,2,1,0. Start should be p-s=4, stop p-e+1 = 1? In Python slice p-s : p-e+1 gives start 4 stop1 empty. Need use `h[p-e : p-s+1][::-1]`? Let's derive: k from s to e increasing, j=p-k from p-s down to p-e. To get increasing slice then reverse, take `h[p-e : p-s+1][::-1]`. For s=0,e=4,p=4: h[0:5][::-1] gives [4,3,2,1,0] correct. For p=0,s=-4,e=0: p-e=0, p-s+1=5 => h[0:5][::-1] [0..4] but expected j=0? For p=0 ks=-4..0: j=0-(-4)=4 down to0. h[0:5][::-1] = [4,3,2,1,0], correct. So use `h[p-e : p-s+1][::-1]`.
For minus_inc: k from s to e, j=p+k from p+s to p+e increasing: `h[p+s : p+e+1]`.
For minus_dec: j=p-k decreasing: `h[p-e : p-s+1][::-1]`.
Plus_inc: j=p+k increasing: `h[p+s : p+e+1]`.
Plus_dec: 1 - same reversed.

`solve_segment` implementation:
```
def solve_segment(A, c_seg, diag_off, h0, unaff, iters):
    A_lin=A.copy(); A_lin[diag_off]=0.0
    c_diag=c_seg[diag_off]; A_diag=A[diag_off]
    lo_all=-h0; hi_all=1.0-h0
    # if h0 near? assume not
    Llo=unaff
    Lhi=max(unaff, float(np.max(c_seg)), 0.0)
    if Lhi <= Llo + 1e-11: return 0.0
    pos=A_lin>1e-13; neg=A_lin<-1e-13; zero=np.abs(A_lin)<=1e-13
    # to avoid repeated array indexing, store pos_c=c_seg[pos], etc.
    for _ in range(iters):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       lo=lo_all; hi=hi_all
       feas=(L >= unaff - 1e-12)
       if feas and zero.any():
          if float(c_seg[zero].max()) > L + 1e-12: feas=False
       if feas and pos.any():
          u=float(np.max((L - c_seg[pos]) / A_lin[pos]))
          if u < hi: hi=u
       if feas and neg.any():
          l=float(np.max((L - c_seg[neg]) / A_lin[neg]))
          if l > lo: lo=l
       if feas:
          if lo > lo_all: lo=max(lo,lo_all)
          if hi < hi_all: hi=min(hi,hi_all)
          if lo > hi + 1e-12: feas=False
       if feas:
          D=A_diag*A_diag - 4.0*(L - c_diag)
          if D >= 0.0:
             rt=np.sqrt(D); r1=0.5*(A_diag-rt); r2=0.5*(A_diag+rt)
             if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
                feas=False
       if feas: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
       if Lhi-Llo < 1e-8: break
    if Lhi <= unaff + 1e-10: return 0.0
    L=Lhi + 1e-10
    lo=lo_all; hi=hi_all
    if pos.any(): hi=min(hi, float(np.max((L - c_seg[pos])/A_lin[pos])))
    if neg.any(): lo=max(lo, float(np.max((L - c_seg[neg])/A_lin[neg])))
    lo=max(lo,lo_all); hi=min(hi,hi_all)
    if lo > hi + 1e-10:
       # fallback: maybe delta 0 feasible; else small step toward reducing max? Return 0
       return 0.0
    D=A_diag*A_diag - 4.0*(L - c_diag)
    if D < 0.0:
       a,b=lo,hi
       # choose point in [a,b] closest to 0
       if a <= 0 <= b: return 0.0
       return a if abs(a)<abs(b) else b
    rt=np.sqrt(D); r1=0.5*(A_diag-rt); r2=0.5*(A_diag+rt)
    best_delta=0.0; best_len=-1.0
    if lo <= r1:
       a=lo; b=min(hi,r1)
       if b>=a-1e-12:
          # choose closest to 0 in [a,b]
          if a <= 0 <= b: cand=0.0
          elif abs(a)<abs(b): cand=a
          else: cand=b
          length=b-a
          if length>best_len: best_len=length; best_delta=cand
    if hi >= r2:
       a=max(lo,r2); b=hi
       if b>=a-1e-12:
          if a <=0 <= b: cand=0.0
          elif abs(a)<abs(b): cand=a
          else: cand=b
          length=b-a
          if length>best_len: best_len=length; best_delta=cand
    # if best_delta 0 but current not feasible? maybe pick endpoint
    if best_len < 0:
       # try midpoint clipped
       best_delta = 0.0 if lo <=0<=hi else (lo+hi)/2
    # Clamp
    if best_delta < lo_all: best_delta=lo_all
    if best_delta > hi_all: best_delta=hi_all
    return best_delta
```
The bisection feasibility loop: For linear constraints, if A>0 δ ≤ (L-c)/A; the maximum upper bound should be max? Actually we need hi ≤ min_i (L-c_i)/A_i for all pos. We initialized hi and set `hi = u` if u<hi where u=max. Wait if need hi ≤ min over constraints, and current hi maybe bound. For each pos constraint gives upper bound u_i. The tightest upper is min_i u_i. If we only take max, that's wrong! Let's correct.
For A>0: δ ≤ u_i. hi = min(hi, min_i u_i). So need `u = float(np.min((L-c_pos)/A_pos))`; if u < hi: hi=u.
For A<0: δ ≥ l_i; lo = max(lo, max_i l_i). Use np.max. Good. I mistakenly used max for pos. Fix.
Zero require all c_i≤L.
For neg, if A<0, (L-c)/A could be negative; need lower bound max. Good.
In final selection, for pos use np.min, neg np.max.
Bisection initial Lhi feasible at δ=0. But if constraints produce hi<lo? Lhi should be feasible; at δ=0, linear constraints for non-diag: if A_i>0, c_i≤Lhi => (Lhi-c_i)/A_i≥0; hi≥0. If A_i<0: (Lhi-c_i)/A_i≤0? Since Lhi≥c_i, numerator ≥0, divided negative ≤0, lower bound ≤0; lo≤0. Diag quadratic at δ=0 feasible. So okay.

But zero mask: If A_lin=0 but c_i > L, infeasible. We check max.

Diagonal quadratic feasibility: Our A_diag includes linear coefficient from both terms. q(δ)=c_diag + A_diag δ - δ^2. Feasible q≤L. We derived D = A^2 -4(L-c). If D>=0, forbidden interval (r1,r2), where r are roots of δ^2 - Aδ + (L-c)=0. If [lo,hi] entirely inside (r1,r2), infeasible. Condition lo > r1 and hi < r2. Good. If D<0, q≤L always? If L<c, L-c negative, D> positive, roots real, forbidden interval. D<0 only if L > c + A^2/4, so diagonal constraint easy, q≤L for all δ. Good. So D<0 feasible all.

Final selection: If D<0, any δ in [lo,hi] diagonal feasible. If D>=0, choose outside forbidden.

```
def cd_pass(h,c,iters,deadline,rng,skip_tol=1e-9):
    n=h.size; Lc=2*n-1
    order=list(range(n))
    if rng is not None:
       # maybe deterministic for first pass, random otherwise? Use rng.shuffle
       rng.shuffle(order)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    improved=False
    for p in order:
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       h0=h[p]
       if h0 <= 1e-12 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-12: continue
       k1s=p-n+1; k1e=p; k2s=-p; k2e=n-1-p
       kmin=min(k1s,k2s); kmax=max(k1e,k2e)
       tmin=kmin+n-1; tmax=kmax+n-1
       unaff=0.0
       if tmin>0: unaff=float(np.max(c[:tmin]))
       if tmax+1<Lc:
           m=float(np.max(c[tmax+1:])); unaff=max(unaff,m)
       if unaff >= cmax - 1e-9:
           # cannot lower global max if an unaffected max remains
           continue
       A=cd_coefficients(p,h) maybe already have kmin? We can reuse function but recalculates.
```

`cd_optimize`:
```
def cd_optimize(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng=None,iters=24):
    h=project_feasible(np.array(h,dtype=float).copy(),n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       h_pass,c,cmax,improved = cd_pass(h,c,iters,deadline,rng)
       # project and recompute
       h_proj=project_feasible(h_pass,n)
       # if projection changed a lot? 
       if np.allclose(h_proj,h_pass,rtol=0,atol=1e-12):
           c_proj=c # maybe still feasible? sum maybe not exact? But cd_pass changes sum; allclose false if sum drift.
       c_proj=np.correlate(h_proj,1-h_proj,'full')
       cur=compute_c5(h_proj,n)
       if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=cur; best_h=h_proj.copy()
       h=h_proj
       if not improved:
          break
    return best_h,best_c
```
But `cd_pass` returns h maybe infeasible; we set h_proj. If improved false, we still project; if cur worse, break. Good.
`cd_pass` returns `h,c` where h infeasible. It uses `c` corresponding to infeasible h. After project, c recompute.
Maybe after projection, cmax could be worse than old feasible best; but we continue from projected. Could get stuck. But okay.
We could if cur > best_c + 1e-7 and no improvement, break.

SLSQP `slsqp_opt`:
```
def slsqp_opt(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    global _N,_ALPHA,_TARGET
    h0=project_feasible(h,n)
    _N=n; _ALPHA=float(alpha); _TARGET=n/2.0
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_fun,'jac':_eq_jac}]
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj, h0, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=_jac, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-10})
       x=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       x=h0
    return x
```
No closures. `_eq_fun` uses `_TARGET`.
Need `_eq_jac` return `np.ones(_N)` but SLSQP may expect array shape (1,m)? It accepts 1D.

def interp_h(h,n_new):
    n_old=len(h)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new,endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h), n_new)
```

def smooth_random(n, rng, scale):
   f=rng.normal(0,1,n)
   # simple moving average circular? Use np.convolve with ones(15) and `mode='same'` causes edge effects; okay.
   k=max(3, n//100)
   f=np.convolve(f, np.ones(k), mode='same')/k
   f=f-f.mean()
   return np.clip(h + scale*f,0,1)

if abs(diff)>1e-12:
   # find index with max room; adjust
   if diff>0: i=np.argmin(h); room=1-h[i]; h[i]=min(1,h[i]+diff)

Add final exact sum fix:
```
def fix_sum(h,n):
  target=n/2
  diff=target-h.sum()
  while abs(diff)>1e-12:
    if abs(diff) < 1e-12/(n+1): break
    if diff>0:
      idx=np.argmin(h)
      room=1-h[idx]
      adj=min(room,diff)
    else:
      idx=np.argmax(h)
      room=h[idx]
      adj=max(-room,diff)
    if abs(adj)<1e-15: break
    h[idx]+=adj
    diff=target-h.sum()
  return h
```
Project already but add.

```
def pg_lse(h,n,alpha,max_iter,init_step,deadline):
  h=project(h,n); f=lse_value(h)
  best=h.copy(), f
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time>deadline: break
    g=lse_grad(h)
    gn=norm(g)
    if gn<1e-10: break
    step=init_step
    imp=False
    for ls in range(30):
       y=project(h - step*g, n)
       fy=lse_value(y)
       if fy < f - 1e-5*step*gn*gn or fy < f - 1e-10:
          h=y; f=fy; imp=True; break
       step*=0.5
    if not imp:
       # try smaller fixed? break
       break
    if f<best: best...
  return best
```

- feasible h.

def pg_lse(h,n,alpha,max_iter,init_step,deadline):
    h=project(h,n); f=lse_value(h,n,alpha); best_h=h.copy(); best_f=f
    step=init_step
    g_prev=None; h_prev=None
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time>deadline: break
       g=lse_grad(h,n,alpha)
       free=(h>1e-6)&(h<1-1e-6)
       gp=g.copy()
       if free.any():
          gp[free]-=g[free].mean()
          gp[~free]=0.0
       else: break
       # If no decrease direction? maybe check max
       d=-gp
       # optional: if norm(d) tiny break
       # BB step update using s,y if available
       if g_prev is not None and h_prev is not None:
          s=h-h_prev; y=g-g_prev; dy=np.dot(s,y)
          if dy>1e-12:
             step= min(10.0, max(1e-4, np.dot(s,s)/dy))
       f_old=f
       # line search
       accepted=False
       for _ in range(20):
          y=project(h + step*d, n)
          fy=lse_value(y,n,alpha)
          if fy < f_old - 1e-10:
             h,y... accepted=True; f=fy; break
          step*=0.5
       if not accepted:
          # maybe try original h? break or reduce step min
          step*=0.1
          if step<1e-6: break
       h_prev=h_name? Need update h_prev after accepted.

def pg_lse_opt(h,n,alphas,iters,deadline):

Let's write lse functions:
```
def lse_value(x,n,alpha):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    m=c.max()
    sh=alpha*(c-m)
    return float(m - np.log(np.exp(sh).sum())/alpha)
```
But if alpha large and sh all -inf? At max sh=0, exp=1, sum≥1.
```
def lse_grad(x,n,alpha):
    c=...
    m=...
    sh=...
    e=np.exp(sh)
    s=e.sum()
    w=e/s
    L=2*n-1
    cw=... etc
    if _ORIENT==0: starts=np.arange(n); ends=starts+n
    else: starts=n-1-np.arange(n); ends=starts+n
    aconst=cw[ends]-cw[starts]
    T1=np.convolve(x,w,mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    T2=np.convolve(x,w[::-1],mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    g=aconst-T1-T2
    return g
```
Need ensure ends within cw length L+1=2n. For D1 ends max (n-1)+n=2n-1, cw length 2n, okay. For D2 starts n-1, ends start+n max 2n-1, okay; starts for p=n-1: 0 end=n.

Implementation of `pg_lse_single` with BB:
```
def pg_lse_single(h,n,alpha,iters,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    f=lse_value(h,n,alpha); best_f=f; best_h=h.copy()
    step=1.0
    h_prev=None; g_prev=None
    for it in range(iters):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       g=lse_grad(h,n,alpha)
       free=(h>1e-7)&(h<1.0-1e-7)
       if not free.any(): break
       gp=g.copy()
       gm=float(np.mean(g[free]))
       gp[free]-=gm
       gp[~free]=0.0
       gd=float(np.dot(gp,gp))
       if gd < 1e-20: break
       # BB if possible
       if g_prev is not None and h_prev is not None:
           s=h-h_prev; y=g-g_prev; dot=float(np.dot(s,y))
           if dot>1e-12:
               step=dot_s_s? np.dot(s,s)/dot
               if not np.isfinite(step) or step<=0: step=0.5*step_old? 
               step=float(np.clip(step,1e-4,10.0))
       # line search
       accepted=False
       s_trial=step
       for _ in range(12):
           y=project_feasible(h - s_trial*gp, n)
           fy=lse_value(y,n,alpha)
           if fy < f - 1e-9:
               # optionally Armijo? not needed
               accepted=True; f=fy; h=y; break
           s_trial*=0.5
       if not accepted:
           # accept if very small? break
           step*=0.25
           if step<1e-6: break
           # try once final
           y=project_feasible(h - step*gp,n); fy=...
           if fy < f - 1e-12: accept else break
       if f < best_f - 1e-12:
           best_f=f; best_h=h.copy()
       if it%20==0:
           cchk=compute_c5(h,n); # could store? but function doesn't track global best exact
       h_prev=h; g_prev=g  # But if accepted, g is old gradient at old h? For BB, y = g_new-g_old. Need update after computing new gradient next iteration. Standard: store h_old and g_old before update; after update, set h_prev=h_old, g_prev? We need s=x_k - x_{k-1}, y=g_k - g_{k-1}. We have g_k at top. We need previous h and previous g from last iteration. At end of loop after updating h to new, store `h_prev = h_old; g_prev = g_old`. But g_old is current g before update. At next iteration, g_new computed, so s = h_new - h_prev, y = g_new - g_prev. Good. So before update: h_old=h; g_old=g. After accepted, set h_prev=h_old; g_prev=g_old.
```
If no accepted, maybe don't update prev.

def pg_lse_opt(h,n,alphas,iters_per_alpha,deadline):
    best_h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    h=best_h.copy()
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time()>deadline-20: break
       h2=pg_lse_single(h,n,alpha,iters_per_alpha,deadline-10)
       c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
       if c2<best_c-1e-12:
          best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); h=h2
       else:
          # maybe continue from h2 even if exact worse? Could help. Choose if LSE better? We don't return LSE. Maybe set h=h2 for next alpha if not much worse.
          h=h2
    return best_h,best_c
```

Tangent function top-level:
```
def tangent_grad(h,g,eps=1e-7):
    n=h.size
    allow=(h>eps)&(h<1-eps)
    allow |= (h<=eps) & (g<=0)
    allow |= (h>=1-eps) & (g>=0)
    gp=g.copy()
    gp[~allow]=0.0
    if allow.any():
       gp[allow] -= np.mean(g[allow])
    return gp
```
Subtract mean of g over allowed, not gp? Yes approximate. If allowed includes bound with g small, okay.
Sum gp=0 over allowed; total sum 0.

Line search in PG:
```
def pg_lse_single(...):
  h=project; f=lse_value; besth=h; bestf=f; step=1.0; x_minus=None; g_minus=None
  for it in range(iters):
    if time>deadline: break
    g=lse_grad(h,n,alpha)
    if x_minus is not None:
       s=h-x_minus; y=g-g_minus; dy=np.dot(s,y)
       if dy>1e-12:
          ss=np.dot(s,s)
          if ss>1e-16:
             step=np.clip(ss/dy,1e-3,5.0)
    gp=tangent_grad(h,g)
    gd=np.dot(gp,gp)
    if gd<1e-18: break
    x0=h; f0=f
    accepted=False
    st=step
    for _ in range(12):
       y=project_feasible(x0 - st*gp,n)
       fy=lse_value(x0? no y)
       if fy < f0 - 1e-9:
          accepted=True; h=y; f=fy; break
       st*=0.5
    if not accepted:
       # try current? break
       break
    if f<bestf-1e-9: bestf=f; besth=h.copy()
    x_minus=x0; g_minus=g
  return besth
```
But BB update uses `h` after accept; x_minus old. At next iteration g new. Good.

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_N = 0
_ALPHA = 1.0
_TARGET = 0.5
_ORIENT = 0

def set_orientation():
    global _ORIENT
    a = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0])
    v = np.array([3.0, 4.0, 5.0])
    c = np.correlate(a, v, mode='full')
    if abs(c[1] - 14.0) <= abs(c[1] - 18.0):
        _ORIENT = 0
    else:
        _ORIENT = 1
```
Arrays length 3 <=32.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    z = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5*n
    if z.size != n: z = z[:n] # not needed
    if np.all(z >= -1e-12) and np.all(z <= 1.0+1e-12):
       zz=np.clip(z,0,1)
       if abs(zz.sum()-target) <= 1e-12:
          return zz
    lo = -float(np.max(z)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(z))
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=float(np.clip(z+mid,0.0,1.0).sum())
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(z+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       for _ in range(20):
          free=(h>1e-14)&(h<1.0-1e-14)
          if not free.any(): break
          add=diff/float(free.sum())
          h[free]=np.clip(h[free]+add,0.0,1.0)
          diff=target-h.sum()
          if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    return h
```
If z contains large values, lo/hi huge; bisection 60 enough. If z.sum at hi maybe not n if hi=1-min(z) makes min=1, max may >1? Actually hi=1-min(z) => min+hi=1, max+hi may >1, clipped sum n? All values >=1? If z has max huge, max+hi >1, clipped to1; min=1, sum n. Good. At lo, max+lo=-1, sum0.

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*(2.0/n))
```

`lse_value`, `lse_grad` as above. Use orientation global.
In lse_grad, if alpha==0? not.
```
def lse_grad(x,n,alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    e = np.exp(alpha*(c-m))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    w = e/s
    L=2*n-1
    cw = np.empty(L+1); cw[0]=0; cw[1:]=np.cumsum(w)
    if _ORIENT == 0:
       st = np.arange(n, dtype=float) # indices int, use int
       en = st + n
    else:
       st = (n - 1) - np.arange(n)
       en = st + n
    aconst = cw[en] - cw[st]
    t1 = np.convolve(x, w, mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    t2 = np.convolve(x, w[::-1], mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    return aconst - t1 - t2
```
Need `st` int array for indexing. Use `np.arange(n)`.
If alpha very high, e mostly zeros except max; s could be >0. Good.

`_obj` for SLSQP:
```
def _obj(x):
    return lse_value(x, _N, _ALPHA)
def _jac(x):
    return lse_grad(x, _N, _ALPHA)
def _eq_fun(x):
    return x.sum() - _TARGET
def _eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones((1, _N))
```

`slsqp_opt` optional.
```
def slsqp_opt(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    global _N, _ALPHA, _TARGET
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    if time.time() > deadline: return h
    _N=n; _ALPHA=float(alpha); _TARGET=0.5*n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_fun,'jac':_eq_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(_obj, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=_jac, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-8})
        xp=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
        xp=h
    return xp
```
No closures.

`tangent_grad` as above.

`pg_lse_single`: Need define top-level. Use `compute_c5` inside? Not needed.
```
def pg_lse_single(h,n,alpha,iters,deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    f = lse_value(h,n,alpha)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_f = f
    step = 1.0
    x_minus = None; g_minus = None
    for it in range(iters):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        g = lse_grad(h,n,alpha)
        if x_minus is not None:
            s = h - x_minus
            y = g - g_minus
            dy = float(np.dot(s,y))
            if dy > 1e-12:
                ss = float(np.dot(s,s))
                if ss > 1e-16:
                    step = float(np.clip(ss/dy, 1e-3, 5.0))
        gp = tangent_grad(h,g)
        gd = float(np.dot(gp,gp))
        if gd < 1e-18: break
        x0 = h; f0 = f; g0 = g
        accepted = False
        st = step
        for _ in range(12):
            y = project_feasible(x0 - st*gp, n)
            fy = lse_value(y,n,alpha)
            if fy < f0 - 1e-9:
                accepted=True; h=y; f=fy; break
            st *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            break
        if f < best_f - 1e-9:
            best_f = f; best_h = h.copy()
        x_minus = x0
        g_minus = g0
    return best_h
```
Potential issue: BB step after no accept not updated. okay.
If `fy` is NaN? No.
Could f decrease but exact C worsen; best_h by LSE. run will choose exact.
Maybe return final h not best_LSE? If final LSE high? We return best LSE. Good.

`pg_lse_opt`:
```
def pg_lse_opt(h,n,alphas,iters,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
       h2 = pg_lse_single(h,n,alpha,iters,deadline-5)
       c2 = compute_c5(h2,n)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
           best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy()
           h=h2
       else:
           # continue if not much worse
           if c2 < best_c + 1e-4:
               h=h2
           else:
               h=best_h
    return best_h,best_c
```
Maybe exact C doesn't improve during alpha but LSE variance better, continue from h2 if c2 close. Good.

`cd_optimize`:
```
def cd_optimize(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng=None,iters=22):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=corr(h); best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       h2,c2,cmax,imp = cd_pass(h,c,iters,deadline,rng,shuffle=(rng is not None and it%2==1))
       h3=project_feasible(h2,n)
       c3=compute_c5(h3,n)
       if c3 < best_c - 1e-12: best_c=c3; best_h=h3.copy()
       h=h3
       if not imp: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
But `cd_pass` returns h2 (infeasible) and c stale. We ignore c2. If projection increases c, best remains. Good.
Could pass `h` if `imp` false? We set h=h3. If h3 worse and imp false, loop breaks. Return best.

`interp_h`:
```
def interp_h(h,n_new):
    n_old=len(h)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new,endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h),n_new)
```

`smooth_perturb`:
```
def smooth_perturb(h,n,rng,scale):
    f = rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    w = max(3, n//80)
    f = np.convolve(f, np.ones(w), mode='same') / float(w)
    f = f - f.mean()
    return project_feasible(h + scale*f, n)
```
`np.ones(w)` algorithmic, w small. Good.

`run`:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    set_orientation()
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n=len(h)
    except Exception:
       n=700
       x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
       h=0.5 + 0.25*np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x + rng.random()*2.0*np.pi)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    # ensure n even? if n odd, maybe use even n+1? But parent even. If fallback n odd, project okay. Could choose 700 even.
    # Phase 1 CD
    cd_deadline = min(deadline - 400, t0 + 250)
    if cd_deadline > t0 + 30:
        h1,c1=cd_optimize(best_h,n,30,cd_deadline,rng,iters=20)
        if c1<best_c: best_c=c1; best_h=h1
    # Phase 2 PG
    pg_deadline = min(deadline - 250, t0 + 700)
    if pg_deadline > t0 + 30:
        h2,c2=pg_lse_opt(best_h,n,[5.0,20.0,80.0,300.0],120,pg_deadline)
        if c2<best_c: best_c=c2; best_h=h2
    # Phase 3 CD
    cd2_deadline = min(deadline - 150, t0 + 950)
    if cd2_deadline > time.time() + 20:
        h3,c3=cd_optimize(best_h,n,60,cd2_deadline,rng,iters=22)
        if c3<best_c: best_c=c3; best_h=h3
    # Phase 4 resolutions
    n_best=len(best_h)
    n_opts=[]
    for nn in (n_best-100, n_best-50, n_best+50, n_best+100, 600, 800):
       if nn>=200 and nn<=1000 and nn!=n_best and (not n_opts or nn not in n_opts): n_opts.append(nn)
    for nn in n_opts:
       if time.time() > deadline - 120: break
       hh=interp_h(best_h,len(best_h),nn)
       # PG short
       if time.time() < deadline - 80:
          hh,cx=pg_lse_opt(hh,nn,[20.0,80.0],60,deadline-60)
       cx=compute_c5(hh,nn)
       if cx < best_c - 1e-9:
          hh,cx=cd_optimize(hh,nn,20,deadline-40,rng,iters=18)
          if cx<best_c: best_c=cx; best_h=hh; n_best=len(best_h)
    # Phase 5 random perturb
    for r in range(6):
       if time.time() > deadline - 70: break
       scale = 0.03*(1.0 - r/6.0) + 0.005
       hh=smooth_perturb(best_h,len(best_h),rng,scale)
       if time.time() < deadline - 50:
          hh,cx=pg_lse_opt(hh,len(best_h),[80.0,300.0],40,deadline-40)
       cx=compute_c5(hh,len(best_h))
       if cx < best_c - 1e-9:
          hh,cx=cd_optimize(hh,len(best_h),15,deadline-30,rng,iters=18)
          if cx<best_c: best_c=cx; best_h=hh
    # optional SLSQP if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 60 and len(best_h) <= 800:
       for alpha in (80.0,300.0):
          if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
          hs=slsqp_opt(best_h,len(best_h),alpha,20,deadline-10)
          cs=compute_c5(hs,len(best_h))
          if cs < best_c - 1e-9:
             hs,cs=cd_optimize(hs,len(best_h),10,deadline-20,rng,iters=18)
             if cs<best_c: best_c=cs; best_h=hs
    # final CD
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
        hf,cfinal=cd_optimize(best_h,len(best_h),100,deadline-2,rng,iters=24)
        if cfinal<best_c: best_c=cfinal; best_h=hf
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,len(best_h))
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_ORIENT = 0

def set_orientation():
    global _ORIENT
    a = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0])
    v = np.array([3.0, 4.0, 5.0])
    c = np.correlate(a, v, mode="full")
    _ORIENT = 0 if abs(c[1] - 14.0) <= abs(c[1] - 18.0) else 1

def c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    z = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * n
    if np.all(z >= -1e-12) and np.all(z <= 1.0 + 1e-12):
        zz = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
        if abs(zz.sum() - target) <= 1e-12:
            return zz
    lo = -float(np.max(z)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(z))
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(z + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(z + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        for _ in range(20):
            free = (h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14)
            if not free.any():
                break
            h[free] = np.clip(h[free] + diff / float(free.sum()), 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - h.sum()
            if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
                break
    return h

def lse_value(x, n, alpha):
    cc = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(np.max(cc))
    e = np.exp(alpha * (cc - m))
    return float(m - np.log(float(np.sum(e))) / alpha)

def lse_grad(x, n, alpha):
    cc = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(np.max(cc))
    e = np.exp(alpha * (cc - m))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    w = e / s
    L = 2 * n - 1
    cw = np.empty(L + 1)
    cw[0] = 0.0
    cw[1:] = np.cumsum(w)
    if _ORIENT == 0:
        st = np.arange(n)
    else:
        st = (n - 1) - np.arange(n)
    en = st + n
    aconst = cw[en] - cw[st]
    t1 = np.convolve(x, w, mode="full")[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    t2 = np.convolve(x, w[::-1], mode="full")[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    return aconst - t1 - t2

def tangent_grad(h, g):
    eps = 1e-7
    allow = (h > eps) & (h < 1.0 - eps)
    allow = allow | ((h <= eps) & (g <= 0.0)) | ((h >= 1.0 - eps) & (g >= 0.0))
    gp = g.copy()
    gp[~allow] = 0.0
    if allow.any():
        gp[allow] -= float(np.mean(g[allow]))
    return gp

def pg_lse_single(h, n, alpha, iters, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    f = lse_value(h, n, alpha)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_f = f
    step = 0.5
    x_minus = None
    g_minus = None
    for it in range(iters):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        g = lse_grad(h, n, alpha)
        if x_minus is not None:
            s = h - x_minus
            y = g - g_minus
            dy = float(np.dot(s, y))
            if dy > 1e-12:
                ss = float(np.dot(s, s))
                if ss > 1e-16:
                    step = float(np.clip(ss / dy, 1e-3, 10.0))
        gp = tangent_grad(h, g)
        gd = float(np.dot(gp, gp))
        if gd < 1e-18:
            break
        x0, f0, g0 = h, f, g
        accepted = False
        st = step
        for _ in range(14):
            y = project_feasible(x0 - st * gp, n)
            fy = lse_value(y, n, alpha)
            if fy < f0 - 1e-9:
                accepted = True
                h = y
                f = fy
                break
            st *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            break
        if f < best_f - 1e-9:
            best_f = f
            best_h = h.copy()
        x_minus = x0
        g_minus = g0
    return best_h

def pg_lse_opt(h, n, alphas, iters, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5(h, n)
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 20:
            break
        h2 = pg_lse_single(h, n, alpha, iters, deadline - 5)
        c2 = c5(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            h = h2
        else:
            h = h2 if c2 < best_c + 1e-4 else best_h
    return best_h, best_c

def cd_coefficients(p, h):
    n = h.size
    k1s, k1e = p - n + 1, p
    k2s, k2e = -p, n - 1 - p
    kmin = min(k1s, k2s)
    kmax = max(k1e, k2e)
    A = np.zeros(kmax - kmin + 1)
    if _ORIENT == 0:
        s, e = max(kmin, k1s), min(kmax, k1e)
        if s <= e:
            A[s - kmin:e - kmin + 1] += 1.0 - h[p - e:p - s + 1][::-1]
        s, e = max(kmin, k2s), min(kmax, k2e)
        if s <= e:
            A[s - kmin:e - kmin + 1] -= h[p + s:p + e + 1]
    else:
        s, e = max(kmin, k2s), min(kmax, k2e)
        if s <= e:
            A[s - kmin:e - kmin + 1] += 1.0 - h[p + s:p + e + 1]
        s, e = max(kmin, k1s), min(kmax, k1e)
        if s <= e:
            A[s - kmin:e - kmin + 1] -= h[p - e:p - s + 1][::-1]
    return kmin + n - 1, kmax + n - 1, A

def solve_segment(A, cseg, diag_off, h0, unaff, iters):
    Al = A.copy()
    Al[diag_off] = 0.0
    Ad = A[diag_off]
    cd = float(cseg[diag_off])
    lo_all, hi_all = -h0, 1.0 - h0
    Llo = unaff
    Lhi = max(unaff, float(np.max(cseg)), 0.0)
    if Lhi <= unaff + 1e-10:
        return 0.0
    pos = Al > 1e-13
    neg = Al < -1e-13
    zero = np.abs(Al) <= 1e-13
    ap, cp = Al[pos], cseg[pos]
    an, cn = Al[neg], cseg[neg]
    cz = cseg[zero]
    for _ in range(iters):
        L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        lo, hi = lo_all, hi_all
        ok = L >= unaff - 1e-11
        if ok and zero.any() and float(np.max(cz)) > L + 1e-11:
            ok = False
        if ok and pos.any():
            hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - cp) / ap)))
        if ok and neg.any():
            lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - cn) / an)))
        if ok:
            lo = max(lo, lo_all)
            hi = min(hi, hi_all)
            if lo > hi + 1e-12:
                ok = False
        if ok:
            D = Ad * Ad - 4.0 * (L - cd)
            if D >= 0.0:
                rt = np.sqrt(D)
                r1 = 0.5 * (Ad - rt)
                r2 = 0.5 * (Ad + rt)
                if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
                    ok = False
        if ok:
            Lhi = L
        else:
            Llo = L
        if Lhi - Llo < 1e-8:
            break
    L = Lhi + 1e-10
    lo, hi = lo_all, hi_all
    if pos.any():
        hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - cp) / ap)))
    if neg.any():
        lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - cn) / an)))
    lo = max(lo, lo_all)
    hi = min(hi, hi_all)
    if lo > hi + 1e-10:
        return 0.0
    D = Ad * Ad - 4.0 * (L - cd)
    best_delta = 0.0
    best_score = 1e100
    if D < 0.0:
        a, b = lo, hi
        if b >= a - 1e-12:
            cand = 0.0 if a <= 0.0 <= b else (a if abs(a) <= abs(b) else b)
            best_delta = cand
            best_score = abs(cand)
    else:
        rt = np.sqrt(D)
        r1 = 0.5 * (Ad - rt)
        r2 = 0.5 * (Ad + rt)
        if lo <= r1:
            a, b = lo, min(hi, r1)
            if b >= a - 1e-12:
                cand = 0.0 if a <= 0.0 <= b else (a if abs(a) <= abs(b) else b)
                if abs(cand) < best_score:
                    best_score = abs(cand)
                    best_delta = cand
        if hi >= r2:
            a, b = max(lo, r2), hi
            if b >= a - 1e-12:
                cand = 0.0 if a <= 0.0 <= b else (a if abs(a) <= abs(b) else b)
                if abs(cand) < best_score:
                    best_score = abs(cand)
                    best_delta = cand
    return float(np.clip(best_delta, lo_all, hi_all))

def cd_pass(h, cc, iters, deadline, rng, shuffle=False):
    n = h.size
    Lc = 2 * n - 1
    order = list(range(n))
    if shuffle and rng is not None:
        rng.shuffle(order)
    cmax = float(np.max(cc))
    improved = False
    ssum = float(h.sum())
    target = 0.5 * n
    for p in order:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h0 = h[p]
        if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-13:
            continue
        k1s, k1e = p - n + 1, p
        k2s, k2e = -p, n - 1 - p
        kmin = min(k1s, k2s)
        kmax = max(k1e, k2e)
        tmin = kmin + n - 1
        tmax = kmax + n - 1
        unaff = 0.0
        if tmin > 0:
            unaff = float(np.max(cc[:tmin]))
        if tmax + 1 < Lc:
            unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(cc[tmax + 1:])))
        if unaff >= cmax - 1e-9:
            continue
        tmin2, tmax2, A = cd_coefficients(p, h)
        if tmin2 != tmin or tmax2 != tmax:
            continue
        cseg = cc[tmin:tmax + 1]
        diag_off = n - 1 - tmin
        if diag_off < 0 or diag_off >= A.size:
            continue
        delta = solve_segment(A, cseg, diag_off, h0, unaff, iters)
        if abs(delta) <= 1e-11:
            continue
        old = cmax
        cc[tmin:tmax + 1] += delta * A
        cc[n - 1] -= delta * delta
        new = float(np.max(cc))
        if new < old - 1e-12:
            h[p] = float(np.clip(h0 + delta, 0.0, 1.0))
            cmax = new
            ssum += delta
            improved = True
            if abs(ssum - target) > 0.05 * n:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                cc = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                cmax = float(np.max(cc))
                break
        else:
            cc[tmin:tmax + 1] -= delta * A
            cc[n - 1] += delta * delta
    return h, cc, improved

def cd_optimize(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng=None, iters=22):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cc = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5(h, n)
    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h2, cc2, imp = cd_pass(h, cc, iters, deadline, rng, (rng is not None and it % 2 == 1))
        h3 = project_feasible(h2, n)
        cur = c5(h3, n)
        if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h3.copy()
        h = h3
        if not imp:
            break
    return best_h, best_c

def interp_h(h, n_new):
    n_old = len(h)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x1, x0, h), n_new)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    set_orientation()
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 8
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h)
        if n > 1000:
            h = interp_h(h, 900)
            n = 900
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h = 0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x + rng.random() * 6.283185307179586)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5(h, n)

    d1 = min(deadline - 380, t0 + 240)
    if d1 > t0 + 30:
        hh, cc = cd_optimize(best_h, n, 35, d1, rng, iters=20)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    d2 = min(deadline - 240, t0 + 720)
    if d2 > time.time() + 40:
        hh, cc = pg_lse_opt(best_h, n, (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0), 140, d2)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    d3 = min(deadline - 140, t0 + 960)
    if d3 > time.time() + 40:
        hh, cc = cd_optimize(best_h, n, 80, d3, rng, iters=22)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    nb = len(best_h)
    for nn in (nb - 100, nb - 50, nb + 50, nb + 100, 600, 800):
        if time.time() > deadline - 120:
            break
        if nn < 200 or nn > 1000 or nn == nb:
            continue
        hh = interp_h(best_h, nb, nn)
        if time.time() < deadline - 70:
            hh, cx = pg_lse_opt(hh, nn, (20.0, 80.0), 50, deadline - 50)
        cx = c5(hh, nn)
        if cx < best_c - 1e-9:
            hh, cx = cd_optimize(hh, nn, 25, deadline - 30, rng, iters=18)
            if cx < best_c:
                best_c = cx
                best_h = hh

    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
        hh, cf = cd_optimize(best_h, len(best_h), 150, deadline - 3, rng, iters=24)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_c = cf
            best_h = hh

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = c5(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
